'days_per_delivery_14_day',\n        'deliveries_per_year_14_day',\n        'decision_threshold_usd',\n    }\n    if set(analysis['assumptions']) != expected_assumptions:\n        raise AssertionError('assumptions block does not have exactly the expected 6 keys')\n    if analysis['assumptions'] != ASSUMPTIONS:\n        raise AssertionError('assumptions block has incorrect values')\n\n    therapy_codes = [therapy['therapy_code'] for therapy in analysis['therapies']]\n    if therapy_codes != sorted(therapy_codes):\n        raise AssertionError('therapies are not sorted by therapy_code')\n\n    for therapy in analysis['therapies']:\n        for key, value in therapy.items():\n            if key in CURRENCY_FIELDS and dec(value) != dec(value).quantize(Decimal('0.01')):\n                raise AssertionError(f'{therapy[\"therapy_code\"]}.{key} is not rounded to 2 decimals')\n\n    expected_totals = {\n        'total_annual_margin_7_day_usd',\n        'total_annual_margin_14_day_usd',\n        'total_annual_margin_difference_14_minus_7_usd',\n        'absolute_total_margin_difference_usd',\n    }\n    if set(analysis['totals']) != expected_totals:\n        raise AssertionError('totals block does not have exactly the expected 4 keys')\n    if set(analysis['recommendation']) != {'decision', 'justification'}:\n        raise AssertionError('recommendation block must have decision and justification')\n\n    per_therapy_diff_sum = money(sum(dec(t['annual_margin_difference_14_minus_7_usd']) for t in analysis['therapies']))\n    total_diff = analysis['totals']['total_annual_margin_difference_14_minus_7_usd']\n    if dec(per_therapy_diff_sum) != dec(total_diff):\n        raise int(row['bag_size_ml']): dec(row['bag_supply_cost_usd'])\n        for row in bag_rows\n    }\n\n    therapy_outputs = []\n    for therapy in therapies:\n        therapy_code = bag size {bag_size_ml}')\n        bag_supply_cost = bag_supply_cost_by_size[bag_size_ml]\n        payment = payments[therapy_code]\n        active_patients_dec = dec(active_patients)\n\n        annual_drug_cost_7 = drug_cost_per_1000_mg * active_patients_dec * dose_mg_per_day * dec(ASSUMPTIONS['days_per_delivery_7_day']) * dec(ASSUMPTIONS['deliveries_per_year_7_day']) / dec(1000)\n        annual_supply_cost_7 = bag_supply_cost * active_patients_dec * dec(ASSUMPTIONS['deliveries_per_year_7_day'])\n        annual_revenue_7 = payment * active_patients_dec * dec(ASSUMPTIONS['deliveries_per_year_7_day'])\n        annual_margin_7 = annual_revenue_7 - annual_drug_cost_7 - annual_supply_cost_7\n\n        annual_drug_cost_14 = drug_cost_per_1000_mg * annual_margin_14 = annual_revenue_14 - annual_drug_cost_14 - annual_supply_cost_14\n        annual_margin_difference = annual_margin_14 - annual_margin_7\n\n        therapy_outputs.append({\n 'annual_drug_cost_14_day_usd': money(annual_drug_cost_14),\n            'annual_supply_cost_14_day_usd': money(annual_supply_cost_14),\n            'annual_revenue_14_day_usd':
